Dual ascent algorithm:
Given f normalized sum=√(2n). M=||f*f||∞. Solve LP: max 1^T g subject to g≥0, (f*g)_k ≤ M for k=0..2n-2. This LP has variable g length n, constraints 2n-1. Since f fixed. The dual: min M ∑ y_k subject to (f*y)_i ≥ 1 for i=0..n-1, y_k≥0, where f*y full length 2n-1 but only first n constraints. Number variables 2n-1, constraints n. Matrix is convolution trapezoidal. If f dense, constraints dense. But maybe can solve LP via scipy with sparse? For n maybe not 6733? We can reduce dimension m, e.g. m=256, run ascent to get good profile, then sample to 6733? Detailed smooth objective:
```
def softmax(y):
    z = y - y.max()
    ax = np.exp(z)
    return ax / ax.sum()

def smooth_loss(y, tau=None):
    a = softmax(y)
    b = np.convolve(a,a, mode='full')
    if tau is None: tau = 50/m? We can set fixed for m=256: tau=1000.
    mx = b.max()
    z = (b - mx)*tau
    s = np.exp(z).sum()
    val = mx + np.log(s)/tau
    w = np.exp(z)/s
    # gradient wrt a
    H = np.zeros(m)
    # H[p] = sum_i a_i w_{p+i}? For smooth max of b_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}; dF/da_p = 2 sum_k w_k a_{k-p} valid.
    # Here H[p] = sum_k w_k a_{k-p} = sum_{i} a_i w_{k?}

LP ascent details with sum=1:
```
def lp_ascent(f, m, max_iter=200, tol=1e-9, time_limit):
    f=f/ f.sum()
    best=f.copy(); best_score=score(m,f)
    for it:
        b=np.convolve(f,f); M=b.max()
        # Build F efficiently
        F=np.zeros((2*m-1,m), float)
        # for i,val in enumerate(f): F[i:i+m,i]=val ; but if val 0 maybe skip? if many zeros still assign.
        for i,val in enumerate(f):
            if val != 0: F[i:i+m,i]=val
        # linprog maximize sum g. c=-1. If M <=0? not.
        res=linprog(-np.ones(m), A_ub=F, b_ub=M, bounds=[(0,None)]*m, method='highs')
        if res.success and res.x.sum()>0: g=res.x/g.sum()
        else: g=f.copy()
        # symmetric average? maybe also reverse g? For LP not.
        # line search
        improved=False
        for t in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0):
            cand=(1-t)*f+t*g; clip; sum; score
            if score<best_score: best=cand; choose cand break
        if no t<1 improves, try smaller 0.01..
        update f=chosen; if f nonfinite fallback.
        if small improvement break.
```

def fgrad(y):
    a = softmax(y)
    b = np.convolve(a,a)
    mx = b.max()
    # tau adaptive maybe tau = max(50, 1000/m?)? Use fixed 1000 for m=256; for m=128 500.

```
def profile_to_length(a, n):
    m=a.size
    if m==n: return a
    # low density values d = m*a (mass per unit length)
    d = m * a
    # cumulative density
    cum = np.insert(np.cumsum(d),0,0.0) # length m+1
    # high boundaries t_i=i/n
    # mass in high cell = ∫_{t_i}^{t_{i+1}} d(step) dt = sum_j d_j * overlap
    # We can compute using cumulative of lower broken: For each high endpoint t, mass from 0 to t in step profile:
    # index j = floor(t*m), rem = t*m - j
    # F(t) = cum[j] + rem*d[j] if j<m else cum[m]
    def F(t): ...

H computation for high using loop:
```
def active_gradient_from_lags(a, k_lags):
    n=a.size; H=np.zeros(n)
    for k in k_lags:
        start = max(0, k - (n-1))
        end = min(n, k+1)
        idx = np.arange(start, end)
        H[idx] += a[k - idx]
    return H
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

def _safe_clip(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)

def _normalize(a, target=1.0):
    a=_safe_clip(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    return (target/s)*a

def _score_exact_list(seq):
    try: v=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception: v=np.inf
    return v

def _fft_conv(a):
    n=a.size; L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<= 1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
For n small, direct conv maybe faster; use direct for size<=2000? Direct np.convolve is optimized. We'll define:
```
def _conv(a):
   return np.convolve(a,a) if a.size <= 1500 else _fft_conv(a)
```

def _upsample(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); m=a.size
   if m==n: return a
   # ensure nonneg sum1
   d = m*a
   cum = np.empty(m+1); cum[0]=0; cum[1:]=np.cumsum(d)
   left = np.arange(n)/n
   right = (np.arange(1,n+1))/n
   def F(t):
      x = t*m
      j = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
      # for t==1, j=m
      j0 = np.clip(j,0,m-1)
      rem = x - np.minimum(j,m-1) ?
```

start=np.clip(a,1e-300,None); start/=start.sum()
tau = min(20000, max(1000, 8.0/ max(np.convolve(start,start).max(),1e-12)))
best_a=start; best_s=fast_score(start)
y0=np.log(start); y0-=y0.mean()
def fgrad(y):
   y=y-y.mean()
   z=y-y.max(); ex=np.exp(z); a=ex/ex.sum()
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   mx=b.max()
   z2=(b-mx)*tau
   s=np.exp(z2).sum()
   val=mx+np.log(s)/tau
   w=np.exp(z2)/s
   H=np.empty(m)
   # Use sliding if possible
   try:
       W=np.lib.stride_tricks.sliding_window_view(w, m)
       H = W @ a
   except Exception:
       for p...
   grad_a=2*H
   mu=np.dot(a, grad_a)
   grad_y=a*(grad_a-mu)
   if not finite: grad_y=0
   # periodic update best? no
   return val, grad_y
res=minimize(fgrad, y0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfunc, 'maxls':40, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7})

# polish with active? maybe done outside.
return a, fast_score(a)
```

def lp_ascent(a, m, max_iter, time_limit, rng):
    f=a.copy(); f/=f.sum()
    best=f; best_s=fast_score(f)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>t0+time_limit: break
       f=f+1e-12*max(f.max(),1); f/=f.sum()

       F=np.zeros((2*m-1,m))
       for i,val in enumerate(f):
           if val>0: F[i:i+m,i]=val
       try res=linprog(-np.ones(m), A_ub=F, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None)*m, method='highs')
       except: res=None
       g=f.copy()
       if res and res.success and res.x is not None:
           gp=res.x; gp[np.isnan]=0; gp=np.clip(gp,0,None); if sum>0: g=gp/g.sum()
       else: g=f+small noise?
       cand = None
       for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0):

           if c.sum()>0: c/=c.sum(); s=fast_score(c)

       if best_s < ... maybe
    return best,best_s

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize = None


def _normalize_positive(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    b = (target / s) * a
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return None
    return b


def _fft_conv(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 1
    need = 2 * n - 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return np.asarray(b[:need], dtype=np.float64)


def _convolution(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1500:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
    return _fft_conv(a)


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    b = _convolution(a)
    mx = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mx / (s * s))


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        v = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
        return v if np.isfinite(v) else float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _upsample_mass(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        b = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        s = b.sum()
        if s > 0:
            return b / s
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n

    b = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = b.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    b = b / s
    cum = np.empty(m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cum[0] = 0.0
    cum[1:] = np.cumsum(b)

    left = np.arange(0, n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    right = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    def F(t):
        x = np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0) * float(m)
        j = np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
        j = np.minimum(j, m - 1)
        rem = x - j
        return cum[j] + rem * b[j]

    mass = F(right) - F(left)
    mass = np.clip(mass, 0.0, None)
    ms = mass.sum()
    if ms <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return mass / ms


def _prototype(length, kind, p1, p2=0.0):
    length = max(2, int(length))
    t = (np.arange(length, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(length)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(length, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "exp":
        beta = float(p1)
        a = np.exp(-beta * t)
    elif kind == "powdec":
        p = float(p1)
        a = np.power(1.0 - t, p)
    elif kind == "rational":
        alpha = float(p1)
        p = float(p2) if p2 != 0 else 2.0
        a = np.power(1.0 + alpha * t, -p)
    elif kind == "sine_exp":
        beta = float(p1)
        eps = float(p2) if p2 != 0 else 0.35
        a = np.exp(-beta * t) * (1.0 + eps * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    elif kind == "sine":
        eps = float(p1)
        a = 1.0 + eps * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    else:
        a = np.ones(length, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-300, None)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return np.ones(length, dtype=np.float64) / length
    return a / s


def _basic_starts(length, parent_profile):
    starts = []
    length = max(4, int(length))
    starts.append(_prototype(length, "uniform", 0.0))
    for beta in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.2, 3.0):
        starts.append(_prototype(length, "exp", beta))
    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        starts.append(_prototype(length, "powdec", p))
    for alpha, p in ((1.0, 1.0), (1.5, 2.0), (2.0, 2.0), (3.0, 2.0), (4.0, 2.0), (6.0, 2.0)):
        starts.append(_prototype(length, "rational", alpha, p))
    for beta, eps in ((0.8, 0.25), (1.0, 0.35), (1.5, 0.45), (2.0, 0.55)):
        starts.append(_prototype(length, "sine_exp", beta, eps))
    for eps in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65):
        starts.append(_prototype(length, "sine", eps))
    if parent_profile is not None and parent_profile.size > 0:
        b = _normalize_positive(parent_profile)
        if b is not None:
            starts.append(_upsample_mass(b, length))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    for scale in (0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4):
        y = rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=length)
        z = y - y.max()
        a = np.exp(z)
        a = np.where(a > 1e-300, a, 1e-300)
        s = a.sum()
        if s > 0:
            starts.append(a / s)

    out = []
    seen = set()
    for x in starts:
        if x is None or x.size != length:
            continue
        y = _normalize_positive(x)
        if y is None:
            continue
        key = tuple(np.round(y, 8).tolist()) if length <= 60 else None
        if key is not None:
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
        out.append(y)
    if not out:
        out.append(_prototype(length, "exp", 1.0))
    return out


def _smooth_optimize(start, length, max_time, maxiter=180):
    start = _normalize_positive(start)
    if start is None:
        return _prototype(length, "exp", 1.0)
    a0 = start.copy()
    b0 = np.convolve(a0, a0)
    tau = float(min(30000.0, max(1000.0, 8.0 / max(float(b0.max()), 1e-12))))

    def fgrad(y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
        y = y - float(y.mean())
        z = y - float(y.max())
        ex = np.exp(z)
        a = ex / ex.sum()

        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        mx = float(b.max())
        z2 = (b - mx) * tau
        s = float(np.exp(z2).sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)
        val = mx + math.log(s) / tau
        w = np.exp(z2) / s

        H = np.empty(length, dtype=np.float64)
        # H[p] = sum_i a_i * w_{p+i}
        for p in range(length):
            H[p] = float(np.dot(a, w[p:p + length]))

        grad_a = 2.0 * H
        mu = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        gy = a * (grad_a - mu)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
            gy = np.zeros_like(y)
        return float(val), gy

    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(start, 1e-300))
    y0 -= y0.mean()

    best_a = start.copy()
    best_s = _fast_score(best_a)

    if minimize is None:
        return best_a

    maxfun = int(max(200, min(2500, 10 * max(1, int(maxiter)))))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fgrad,
            y0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 40,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
            },
        )
        y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            z = y - y.max()
            a = np.exp(z)
            a = np.where(a > 0.0, a, 0.0)
            s = a.sum()
            if s > 0:
                a = a / s
                sc = _fast_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
                    best_a = a
                    best_s = sc
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a


def _active_polish(a, max_time, K=48):
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 4:
        return a if a is not None else np.ones(max(4, int(a.size)), dtype=np.float64)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(best)
    no_improve = 0
    use_fft = best.size > 1500
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max(0.0, float(max_time)) and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        b = _fft_conv(best) if use_fft else np.convolve(best, best)
        b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)) or b.size == 0:
            break

        Kloc = max(8, min(int(K), b.size))
        try:
            if Kloc < b.size:
                idx = np.argpartition(b, -Kloc)[-Kloc:]
            else:
                idx = np.arange(b.size)
        except Exception:
            break

        n = best.size
        H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in idx:
            k = int(k)
            start = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            end = min(n, k + 1)
            if start >= end:
                continue
            ii = np.arange(start, end, dtype=np.int64)
            H[ii] += best[k - ii]

        Hmax = float(H.max())
        if Hmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        improved = False
        for eta in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.5, 2.5):
            cand = best * np.exp(-eta * (H / Hmax))
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
            cs = cand.sum()
            if cs <= 0:
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            sc = _fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                best = cand
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 12 == 0:
                rng = np.random.default_rng((int(time.monotonic() * 1e6) & 0xFFFFFFFF) ^ int(n))
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)
                cand = best * np.exp(noise)
                cand = _normalize_positive(cand)
                if cand is not None:
                    sc = _fast_score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 + 1e-6):
                        best = cand
            if no_improve > 80:
                break

    return best


def _lp_ascent(start, length, max_time, max_iter=120):
    if linprog is None:
        return _smooth_optimize(start, length, max_time, maxiter=80)

    f = _normalize_positive(start)
    if f is None or f.size != length:
        f = _prototype(length, "exp", 1.0)

    best = f.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, float(max_time)) or time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
            break

        fs = f + 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(f.max()))
        fs = fs / fs.sum()
        b = np.convolve(fs, fs)
        M = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            break

        F = np.zeros((2 * length - 1, length), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(length):
            F[i:i + length, i] = fs

        g = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                -np.ones(length),
                A_ub=F,
                b_ub=np.full(2 * length - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * length,
                method="highs",
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and res.x is not None:
                gp = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                gp = np.where(np.isfinite(gp), gp, 0.0)
                gp = np.clip(gp, 0.0, None)
                gs = float(gp.sum())
                if gs > 1e-12:
                    g = gp / gs
        except Exception:
            g = None

        if g is None:
            g = f * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=length))
        g = _normalize_positive(g)
        if g is None:
            g = f.copy()

        improved = False
        for tt in (0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - tt) * f + tt * g
            cand = _normalize_positive(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                best = cand
                best_score = sc
                f = cand
                no_improve = 0
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 40:
                break

    return best


def _all_simple_high(n):
    out = []
    params = (
        ("uniform", 0.0),
        ("exp", 0.5), ("exp", 0.8), ("exp", 1.0), ("exp", 1.3),
        ("exp", 1.7), ("exp", 2.2), ("exp", 3.0),
        ("powdec", 0.25), ("powdec", 0.5), ("powdec", 0.75),
        ("powdec", 1.0), ("powdec", 1.5), ("powdec", 2.0),
        ("rational", (1.0, 1.0)), ("rational", (1.5, 2.0)),
        ("rational", (2.0, 2.0)), ("rational", (3.0, 2.0)),
        ("rational", (4.0, 2.0)), ("rational", (6.0, 2.0)),
        ("sine_exp", (0.8, 0.25)), ("sine_exp", (1.0, 0.35)),
        ("sine_exp", (1.5, 0.45)), ("sine_exp", (2.0, 0.55)),
        ("sine", 0.25), ("sine", 0.4), ("sine", 0.55),
    )
    for p in params:
        out.append(_prototype(n, p[0], *p[1]))
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = t_global + max(0.0, budget - 12.0)
    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(a, exact_now=False):
        a = _normalize_positive(a)
        if a is None or a.size == 0:
            return
        sc_fast = _fast_score(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_fast):
            return
        candidates.append((sc_fast, np.array(a, dtype=np.float64)))
        if len(candidates) > 40:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            del candidates[40:]

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent.sum() <= 0:
                parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and 4 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        n_out = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n_out = 6733
    n_out = max(8, min(20000, n_out))

    if parent is not None:
        pn = _normalize_positive(parent)
        if pn is not None:
            add_candidate(pn, exact_now=True)

    for a in _all_simple_high(n_out):
        add_candidate(a)

    # Lower-resolution global search.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        if budget > 420:
            m_small = 256
        elif budget > 160:
            m_small = 192
        else:
            m_small = 128

        parent_down = None
        if parent is not None:
            parent_norm = _normalize_positive(parent)
            if parent_norm is not None:
                parent_down = _upsample_mass(parent_norm, m_small)

        starts = _basic_starts(m_small, parent_down)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

        # Time-aware start ordering: try a diverse subset.
        if len(starts) > 18:
            starts = starts[::len(starts) // 18 + 1]

        total_opt_time = max(0.0, min(360.0, (_GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic())))
        if total_opt_time >= 5.0:
            per_start = total_opt_time / max(1, len(starts))
            for x in starts:
                if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
                    break
                if per_start < 2.0:
                    continue
                try:
                    prof = _smooth_optimize(x, m_small, max_time=per_start * 0.7, maxiter=140)
                    prof = _active_polish(prof, max_time=per_start * 0.3, K=16)
                    add_candidate(prof)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 60.0:
            if parent_down is not None:
                add_candidate(_upsample_mass(_lp_ascent(parent_down, m_small, 45.0, 80), n_out))
            for beta in (1.0, 2.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 60.0:
                    break
                st = _prototype(m_small, "exp", beta)
                prof = _lp_ascent(st, m_small, 30.0, 70)
                prof = _active_polish(prof, 10.0, K=16)
                add_candidate(_upsample_mass(prof, n_out))

        # Refine the best low-res profiles at output resolution.
        if candidates and time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 30.0:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            refined = []
            for sc, prof in candidates:
                if prof.size == n_out:
                    refined.append(prof.copy())
                else:
                    refined.append(_upsample_mass(prof, n_out))
                if len(refined) >= 5:
                    break

            remaining = max(0.0, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
            if remaining > 10.0:
                share = remaining / max(1, len(refined))
                for rp in refined:
                    if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 20.0:
                        break
                    gp = _active_polish(rp, max_time=share, K=48)
                    add_candidate(gp)

    # Final verification with authoritative evaluator.
    candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    best_seq = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # Always have one known fallback.
    fallback = _prototype(n_out, "exp", 1.0)
    if fallback is not None:
        exact = _exact_score(fallback)
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            best_seq = fallback
            best_exact = exact

    n_eval = min(16, len(candidates))
    for i in range(n_eval):
        if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 4.0:
            break
        _, seq = candidates[i]
        if seq is None or seq.size == 0:
            continue
        if best_seq is not None and seq.size == best_seq.size:
            # Cheap identity check for obvious duplicates at final scale.
            if np.linalg.norm(seq - best_seq) <= 1e-10:
                continue
        sc = _exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = fallback
    best_seq = _normalize_positive(best_seq)
    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = _prototype(n_out, "exp", 1.0)
        if best_seq is None:
            best_seq = np.ones(n_out, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_out)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    if any((not math.isfinite(x)) or x < 0.0 or x > 1000.0 for x in out):
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) if math.isfinite(x) else 0.0 for x in out]
    if not out or sum(out) <= 1e-12:
        out = [1.0 / n_out] * n_out
    return out
```
